event = {
	id = 102
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { local_flag = AllDefense }
		OR = {
			policy = { type = professional_army value = 2 } #Not demilitarized
			NOT = { local_flag = demilitarized }
		}
		OR = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { country = JAP }
			month = 1 #delay for AI japan, so that it doesn't deploy only militia to Shanghai
		}
	}
	
	name = EVT_102_NAME
	desc = EVT_102_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = march year = 1963 }

	action = {

#rebel init
event = {
	id = 104
	random = no
	country = REB 
	
	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = REBInit }
	}
	
	name = "AI_EVENT"
	style = 2

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = march year = 1963 }

	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = REBInit }
		command = { type = plain_move 		which = land value = 50 }
		command = { type = desert_move 		which = land value = 50 }
		command = { type = mountain_move 	which = land value = 50 }
		command = { type = urban_move 		which = land value = 50 }
		command = { type = hill_move 		which = land value = 50 }
		command = { type = forest_move 		which = land value = 50 }
		command = { type = jungle_move 		which = land value = 50 }
		command = { type = swamp_move 		which = land value = 50 }
		
		#Defense buffs
		command = { type = defensiveness 		which = land value = -5  }
		command = { type = plain_defense 		which = land value = -10 }	#-40
		command = { type = desert_defense 		which = land value = 10  }	#-20
		command = { type = mountain_defense		which = land value = 150 }	#+70
		command = { type = urban_defense		which = land value = 100 }	#+50
		command = { type = hill_defense			which = land value = 30  }	#0
		command = { type = forest_defense 		which = land value = 80  }	#+50
		command = { type = jungle_defense 		which = land value = 80  }	#+50
		command = { type = swamp_defense 		which = land value = 200 }	#+100
		
		command = { type = shore_attack 		which = land value = -20 }
		command = { type = river_attack 		which = land value = -20 }
		
		command = { type = plain_attack 		which = land value = -23 }	#-15
		command = { type = desert_attack 		which = land value = -16 }	#-12
		command = { type = mountain_attack 		which = land value =  4  }	#+8
		command = { type = urban_attack 		which = land value =  1  }	#+5
		command = { type = hill_attack 			which = land value = -4  }	#0
		command = { type = forest_attack 		which = land value =  1  }	#+5
		command = { type = jungle_attack 		which = land value =  1  }	#+5
		command = { type = swamp_attack 		which = land value =  5  }	#+15
		
		command = { type = ground_def_eff	when  = 0 		value = 0.80 }
		command = { type = max_organization which = land	value = 10	 }
		command = { type = morale 			which = land 	value = 20	 }
		
		command = { type = ai which = "default.ai" }
	}
}

#belligerence lower limit

event = {
	id = 105
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	
	trigger = {
		local_flag = AllInit
		NOT = { ispuppet = yes }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 2 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 2	} }
			}
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 3 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 5	} }
			}
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 4 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 10	} }
			}
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 5 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 30	} }
			}
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 6 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 45	} }
			}
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 7 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 55	} }
			}
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 8 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 63	} }
			}
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 9 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 69	} }
			}
			AND = {
				policy = { type = professional_army value = 10 }
				NOT = { belligerence = { value = 73	} }
			}
		}
	}
	
	name = EVT_105_NAME
	desc = EVT_105_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = march year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_OK
		command = { type = belligerence value = -100 } #first, reset
		command = { type = belligerence value = 3 } #3 extra as buffer, to make sure the event doesn't fire every 15 days
		command = { type = trigger which = 101 } #The belligerence assignment command is not stable if given directly inside the triggerable event, have to separate it into its own event :(
	}
}

		command = { type = set_domestic which = freedom			value = 1  }
		command = { type = set_domestic which = free_market		value = 1  }
		command = { type = set_domestic which = interventionism	value = 3  }
	}
